async function saveImages(items                 , write            )                  {
	const labels = new Map                         ();
	const label = async ({ data, mimeType }              , index        )                  => {
		const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(data), (char) => char.charCodeAt(0));
		const kind = `${mimeType}, ${bytes.length} bytes`;
		// A failed write must not discard the result of a script whose calls already ran: it becomes part of the label.
		const saved = await write(`image-${index}${IMAGE_EXTENSIONS[mimeType] ?? ".bin"}`, bytes);
		return "path" in saved
			? `[Image saved to ${saved.path} (${kind})]`
			: `[Image (${kind}) could not be saved: ${saved.error}]`;
	};
	const result = await Promise.all(
		items.map(async (item)                  => {
			if (item.type !== "image") return [item];
			let pending = labels.get(item.data);
			if (pending === undefined) {
				pending = label(item, labels.size + 1);
				labels.set(item.data, pending);
			}
			return [{ type: "text", text: await pending }, item];
		}),
	);
	return result.flat();
}

function formatError(
	result                                        ,
	calls                                             ,
) {
	const { error } = result;
	const head =
		error.kind === "script"
			? (error.stack ?? `${error.name ?? "Error"}: ${error.message}`)
			: error.kind === "timeout"
				? `Script timed out: ${error.message}`
				: error.kind === "aborted"
					? `Script aborted: ${error.message}`
					: `Script sandbox failed: ${error.message}`;
	const summary =
		calls.length === 0
			? "No tool calls were made."
			: `Tool calls made before the failure (they are not undone): ${calls.map((call) => `${call.name} (${call.status})`).join(", ")}`;
	return `${head}\n\n${summary}`;
}

/** The tool result's content for a script's result, and the path of the complete text when it was cut. */
       async function formatResult(
	result                ,
	options   
		                                                                                                  
		                                 
		                        
		                                 
		                                                            
		                        
		                           
	 ,
)                                                        {
	const output = [...result.output];
	if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) output.push({ type: "text", text: valueText(result.value) });
	const items = formatItems(output);
	if (!result.ok) items.push({ type: "text", text: `Script error:\n${formatError(result, options.calls)}` });
	const generated = options.generatedImages;
	if (generated > 0 && !items.some((item) => item.type === "image")) {
		items.push({
			type: "text",
			text: `Note: models.generateImages() returned ${generated} image${generated === 1 ? "" : "s"} that the script did not show. Show each image block of result.output with image(block).`,
		});
	}
	const truncated = await codemodeOutputtruncate(joinAdjacentText(items), options.maxOutputTokens, options.limits, options.write);
	// After truncation, which joins the text items and moves images after them, so each path stays next to its image.
	const content = joinAdjacentText(await saveImages(truncated.items, options.write));
	const header = `${result.ok ? "Script completed" : "Script failed"}\nWall time ${(options.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)} seconds\nOutput:\n`;
	return {
		content: [{ type: "text", text: header }, ...content],
		...(truncated.fullOutputPath === undefined ? {} : { fullOutputPath: truncated.fullOutputPath }),
	};
}

// ══ src/codemode/codemode.ts ══
/**
 * Durable code mode: a tool that runs model-written JavaScript in a QuickJS sandbox, whose script survives a restart.
 *
 * Problem: a script is in-memory VM state. A restart while
 *
 *     const results = await Promise.all(paths.map((path) => tools.read({ path })));
 *
 * waits for the last of 200 reads would lose the script and every read it made.
 *
 * Solution: deterministic replay (`CodemodeSandboxBase.start()` in pi-codemode). The VM only runs when the host hands
 * it a result; given the same start data, clock, seed, and results in the same order, it makes the same calls with
 * the same arguments. The tool is replay-safe: after a restart, `execute()` runs again, starts the script again from
 * the stored start data, and hands it the results it got before, in the order it got them, from a journal written
 * before each delivery. Nested tool calls reattach to their tasks by key (`c<VM call id>`), so they never run twice
 * unless their own replay policy reruns them; their results come from `NestedCallDoc`. Then the script continues live.
 *
 * Documents, scoped to the code mode call's task and gone when it settles:
 * - `pi.codemode.run`: the start data, written once before the VM starts, and the outcome, written once when the call
 *   decides it (with the store writes, in one transaction), so a rerun after that returns it without a VM.
 * - `pi.codemode.journal`: one entry per delivered result, in delivery order: the VM call ID, the clock value, the
 *   calls the VM had issued after processing it (once acknowledged), and a global's outcome, which nothing else stores.
 *
 * Each result's acknowledged call count is recorded with the next journal commit, or at once when the VM has processed
 * everything delivered: a script waiting on a long call is where a restart usually finds it. A sequential script
 * therefore costs one journal commit and one count commit per result, on top of its nested calls' own; that is the
 * price of checking the most common crash point.
 *
 * A call is launched only once the count of calls issued in the drain that made it is committed. So every key an earlier
 * attempt admitted is at most its last recorded count; a replay checks every recorded count, so when it releases its
 * calls it has made every call an earlier attempt could have admitted, and later calls are new.
 *
 * A rerun refuses to replay (no new nested calls; nested calls admitted earlier may still resume on their own and are
 * aborted by the call's cleanup) when this module's version or the VM's fingerprint changed, when the deadline passed,
 * or when the script diverges: it makes another call, or calls in another order, than the journal says. Each ends the
 * call with an error result.
 */
                                                                               
                                                         
        
                       
                      
                     
                  
                          
                      
                                              
                                                                                                  
                                            
                                            
                                                    
                                            
                                                                            
                                                                       
             
           
                           
                  
                     
                  
                             
                                             
                                              
        
                        
                     
                 
                                
             
          
                       
        
                 
                          
             
                  
                      
                            
             
                     
                                                                                       

/**
 * Version of everything in this module that turns stored results into what a script sees: result mapping, globals,
 * start data. Bump it when that changes, so a call started before an upgrade is not replayed by different code.
 */
       const CODEMODE_MODULE_VERSION = 2;

       const CODEMODE_TOOL_NAME = "codemode";

/** One tool as the script sees it: `tools.<jsName>`. */
/**
 * `structured`: the tool declares `structuredOutputSchema`, so an error result with a value resolves with it.
 * `hidden`: an internal tool of this extension (`pi.codemode.*`), which scripts reach only through a global such as
 * `models.classify()`; never in `tools`, `ALL_TOOLS`, or tool search.
 */
                                                                                                            

/** What a call starts with; a rerun starts the VM from exactly this. */
              
	                
	                  
	             
	                                                                                   
	                     
	                                                        
	                  
	                      
	                                                                                          
	                                                                                             
	                                             
	                                                                        
	                        
	                                 
  

                 
	              
	                                                                                                                    
	                   
	                                 
	                    
  

       const CodemodeRunDoc = defineDoc          ({
	kind: "pi.codemode.run",
	version: 1,
	scope: "task",
	initial: () => ({}),
});

                     
	                                                                        
	           
	                                                   
	           
	                                                                                       
	                     
	                                                                         
	                                                                 
  

                     
	                        
	                                                                                                          
	                     
  

       const CodemodeJournalDoc = defineDoc              ({
	kind: "pi.codemode.journal",
	version: 1,
	scope: "task",
	initial: () => ({ entries: [] }),
});

/**
 * What scripts keep with `store()` and read with `load()`, per conversation. Rewindable and copied as of the fork
 * point, so a fork sees the values as they were when its entry was committed.
 */
       const CodemodeStoreDoc = defineDoc                                       ({
	kind: "pi.codemode.store",
	version: 1,
	scope: "conversation",
	history: "rewindable",
	fork: "asOf",
	initial: () => ({ values: {} }),
});

/** One tool call a script made, as the code mode call's details show it. */
                                  
	                                                                            
	           
	             
	                                                      
	             
	                                                 
	                    
	                                   
	               
  

/**
 * The code mode call's details, in its result: its tool calls in the order it made them, with their status. Calls to
 * globals are not listed. While the script runs, UIs show its nested calls from `pi.live.nestedTools`, so the list is
 * written once, with the result.
 */
                                   
	                            
	                                                                                            
	                        
  

const ARGS_PREVIEW_CHARS = 200;
const ERROR_PREVIEW_CHARS = 500;

function cut(text        , max        )         {
	return text.length > max ? `${text.slice(0, max - 3)}...` : text;
}

/** A function scripts call as a top-level global; its result is journaled. */
                              
	                      
	                          
	                                                                                                
  

const codemodeSchema = Type.Object({ code: Type.String({ description: "Raw JavaScript source." }) });

                                                              

                                   
	                                                                                          
	                                      
	                                                                                                       
	                                                   
	                                                                                              
	                               
	                                                                                                   
	                          
	                                                        
	                                             
  

/** Outputs bounded by the script's own token budget; the Harness's limits only stop a runaway. */
const OUTPUT_LIMITS = { maxBytes: 4 * 1024 * 1024, maxLines: 100_000 }         ;

/** Globals for finding tools, answered from the start data, so a replay sees what the first run saw. */
const DISCOVERY_GLOBALS = ["searchTools", "describeTool", "describeNamespace"]         ;
const DEFAULT_SEARCH_LIMIT = 8;

/**
 * Whether `query` names `namespace`: its name or one of its `aliases`, as written or as the same script identifier
 * (upstream `isNamespaceName`: `dev-radius` and `dev_radius` are both `dev_radius`). This is the lookup
 * `describeNamespace()` and the `namespace` option of `searchTools()` use, so an MCP namespace registered with
 * `aliases: [server]` is found by the server name.
 */
function matchesNamespace(namespace                 , query        )          {
	const names = [namespace.name, ...(Array.isArray(namespace.aliases) ? namespace.aliases : [])];
	return names.some((name) => name === query || toCodemodeIdentifier(name) === toCodemodeIdentifier(query));
}

/** The code mode tool. Scripts call the conversation's callable tools as `tools.<name>(args)`. */
       function createCodemodeTool(options                     )                                          {
	return defineTool({
		name: CODEMODE_TOOL_NAME,
		description: codemodeDescription(options.models !== false),
		parameters: codemodeSchema,
		outputLimits: OUTPUT_LIMITS,
		// Capable models write the script as raw text instead of a JSON-escaped string; pi-ai applies it only for them.
		constrainedSampling: { type: "grammar", variants: { openai_lark: CODEMODE_SOURCE_GRAMMAR } },
		callers: ["model"],
		replay: "safe",
		execute: (args, api, context) => new CodemodeCall(options, api, context).execute(args.code),
	});
}

/**
 * The `codemode` extension: the code mode tool, and the `codemode` prompt section listing the tools scripts can call.
 */
       function createCodemodeExtension(options                     )            {
	return defineExtension({
		name: "codemode",
		tools: [createCodemodeTool(options), ...(options.models === false ? [] : createModelTools())],
		sections: [
			section("codemode", (input) =>
				codemodeSection(
					// Deferred tools are found with `searchTools()`, as the coding agent leaves them out too.
					input.agent.callable.filter(
						(tool) => !tool.name.startsWith(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX) && tool.deferred !== true,
					),
					options.namespaces ?? [],
					options.inlineBudget ?? DEFAULT_CODEMODE_INLINE_BUDGET,
				),
			),
		],
	});
}

/**
 * What a script gets for a nested call, as in the coding agent. A tool with a schema resolves with its value, also on
 * an error result that carries one (`bash`'s `{ output, exitCode }` for a nonzero exit); a schema-less tool
 * resolves with its text output, images left out, as its declaration says. Any other error rejects, with its text: a
 * schema-less tool's output, else its error diagnostics.
 */
function outcomeOf(name        , structured         , result                           )                  {
	const value = structured ? result.structuredOutput : textOf(result.structuredOutput);
	if (!result.isError || (structured && value !== undefined)) return { ok: true, value };
	const message = structured ? "" : (value          );
	const diagnostics = result.diagnostics
		.filter((diagnostic) => diagnostic.severity === "error")
		.map((diagnostic) => diagnostic.message)
		.join("\n");
	return {
		ok: false,
		message: message || diagnostics || `Tool ${name} failed`,
		...(value === undefined ? {} : { properties: { structuredOutput: value } }),
	};
}

/** The text of a schema-less tool's collapsed output: a string, one image, or an array of text and image blocks. */
function textOf(value                       )         {
	if (typeof value === "string") return value;
	if (!Array.isArray(value)) return "";
	return value.flatMap((item) => (isText(item) ? [item.text] : [])).join("\n");
}

function isText(item         )                      {
	return typeof item === "object" && item !== null && (item               ).type === "text";
}

function errorResult(message        )                      {
	return { output: [{ type: "text", text: message }], isError: true };
}

/** One execution of the code mode tool: a first run, or a rerun after a restart that replays the journal. */
class CodemodeCall {
	                 options                     ;
	                 api                  ;
	                 context         ;
	                 controller = new AbortController();
	        run              ;
	        start        ;
	/** The journal as this attempt found it; replay delivers exactly these. */
	        replayed                 = [];
	/** Entries in the journal, replayed and live; the next delivery is `length + 1`. */
	        journalLength = 0;
	/** Replayed entries delivered so far. */
	        nextReplayed = 0;
	        replaying = false;
	/** Whether an earlier attempt ran this call: its nested call keys may already exist. */
	        rerun = false;
	/** Whether live calls may launch; before, they are held (see `release()`). */
	        released = false;
	/** The count an earlier attempt recorded after the script's first run. */
	        firstIssued                    ;
	/** The highest issued count known to be committed: calls up to it may launch. */
	        durableIssued = 0;
	/** Live calls waiting for their issued count to be committed. */
	        waiting                     = [];
	/** Journal index of each replayed call ID. */
	        replayIndex = new Map                ();
	/** Calls the VM issued so far; its call IDs are 1, 2, ... */
	        issued = 0;
	/** Replay: results of journaled calls, ready to deliver in journal order. */
	                 ready = new Map                         ();
	/** Replay: calls the journal does not have, admitted once replay is done. */
	        held                     = [];
	/** Live: finished calls waiting for their journal commit. */
	        queue                                                                                          = [];
	/** Acknowledged counts to record, by result number (0: the script's first run). */
	                 acks = new Map                ();
	        committing = false;
	/** Set once the call decided an outcome other than the script's own (divergence, mismatch) or failed. */
	        ended                                 ;
	        failure         ;
	/** `models.classify()`/`generateImages()` calls in flight, and those waiting for a slot. */
	        modelCalls = 0;
	                 modelWaiters                 = [];
	/** Images `models.generateImages()` returned, to notice a script that never shows them. */
	        generatedImages = 0;
	/** The script's tool calls for `details`, by VM call ID. */
	                 rows = new Map                            ();
	/** Resolves once the VM's fingerprint is checked or recorded; nothing is delivered or admitted before. */
	        gate                ;
	        openGate             ;

	constructor(options                     , api                  , context         ) {
		this.options = options;
		this.api = api;
		this.context = context;
		this.gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
			this.openGate = resolve;
		});
	}

	async execute(source        )                               {
		const { code, options: sourceOptions } = parseCodemodeSource(source);
		const { api, context } = this;
		const agent = await api.agent(context);
		const tools                 = agent.callable.map((tool) => ({
			name: tool.name,
			jsName: toCodemodeIdentifier(tool.name),
			description: sampleOf(tool),
			...(tool.structuredOutputSchema === undefined ? {} : { structured: true          }),
			...(tool.name.startsWith(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX) ? { hidden: true          } : {}),
		}));
		const callable = agent.callable.filter((tool) => !tool.name.startsWith(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX));
		const models = this.options.models === false ? [] : [...CATALOG_GLOBALS, ...Object.keys(MODEL_CALLS)];
		const globals = [
			...DISCOVERY_GLOBALS.map((name) => ({ name, spread: true })),
			...models.map((name) => ({ name, spread: true })),
			...(this.options.globals ?? []).map((global) => ({ name: global.name, spread: global.spread === true })),
		];
		const namespaces                      = [];
		for (const tool of callable) {
			const namespace = namespaceOf(tool.name, this.options.namespaces ?? []);
			if (namespace === undefined) continue;
			let entry = namespaces.find((candidate) => candidate.name === namespace.name);
			if (entry === undefined) {
				entry = {
					name: namespace.name,
					...(namespace.aliases === undefined ? {} : { aliases: namespace.aliases }),
					...(namespace.description === undefined ? {} : { description: namespace.description }),
					...(namespace.instructions === undefined ? {} : { instructions: namespace.instructions }),
					tools: [],
				};
				namespaces.push(entry);
			}
			entry.tools.push(tool.name);
		}
		const now = Date.now();
		const seed = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] ;
		            
			                               
			                                                                                             
		const found = await api.commit(async (tx)                 => {
			const run = await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, api.taskId);
			if (run.result !== undefined) return { done: copyJson(run.result             )                        };
			const rerun = run.start !== undefined;
			if (run.start === undefined) {
				const store = await tx.doc(CodemodeStoreDoc, api.conversationId);
				const timeout = sourceOptions.timeoutMs;
				run.start = {
					version: CODEMODE_MODULE_VERSION,
					startedAt: now,
					seed,
					...(timeout === undefined ? {} : { deadline: now + timeout }),
					tools,
					namespaces,
					globals,
					maxOutputTokens: sourceOptions.maxOutputTokens ?? DEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
					store: copyJson(store.values             )                             ,
				};
			}
			const journal = await tx.doc(CodemodeJournalDoc, api.taskId);
			return {
				start: copyJson(run.start                        )                    ,
				journal: copyJson(journal.entries                        )                             ,
				firstIssued: journal.firstIssued,
				rerun,
			};
		}, context);
		if ("done" in found) return found.done;
		this.start = found.start;
		this.replayed = found.journal;
		this.replayIndex = new Map(found.journal.map((entry, index) => [entry.id, index]));
		this.journalLength = found.journal.length;
		this.replaying = found.journal.length > 0;
		this.rerun = found.rerun;
		this.firstIssued = found.firstIssued;
		// A loop, not spread arguments: a journal can hold more entries than a call takes arguments.
		this.durableIssued = found.journal.reduce(
			(max, entry) => Math.max(max, entry.issuedAfter ?? 0),
			found.firstIssued ?? 0,
		);

		if (this.start.version !== CODEMODE_MODULE_VERSION) {
			return this.decide(
				errorResult(
					`The script cannot resume: it started under code mode version ${this.start.version}, this is ${CODEMODE_MODULE_VERSION}.`,
				),
			);
		}
		const remaining = this.start.deadline === undefined ? undefined : this.start.deadline - Date.now();
		if (remaining !== undefined && remaining <= 0) {
			return this.decide(errorResult(`Script timed out: its deadline passed while it was interrupted.`));
		}

		const onAbort = () => this.controller.abort(context.abortSignal?.reason);
		context.abortSignal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
		if (context.abortSignal?.aborted) onAbort();
		try {
			this.run = this.options.sandbox.start(code, {
				startedAt: this.start.startedAt,
				seed: this.start.seed,
				tools: this.start.tools.filter((tool) => tool.hidden !== true),
				globals: this.start.globals,
				store: this.start.store,
				signal: this.controller.signal,
				...(remaining === undefined ? {} : { timeoutMs: remaining }),
				onStarted: (fingerprint) => void this.checkFingerprint(fingerprint),
				onCall: (call) => this.onCall(call),
				onIdle: (seq, issued) => this.onIdle(seq, issued),
			});
			const result = await this.run.result;
			if (this.failure !== undefined) throw this.failure;
			if (this.ended !== undefined) return this.ended;
			context.abortSignal?.throwIfAborted();
			// Aborted, but not by this call: the host closed the sandbox, for example while shutting down before closing the
			// Harness. That decides nothing about the script: store nothing and end with this invocation, so a later attempt
			// replays it.
			if (!result.ok && result.error.kind === "aborted" && !this.controller.signal.aborted) {
				await new Promise      ((resolve) => {
					if (context.abortSignal === undefined || context.abortSignal.aborted) resolve();
					else context.abortSignal.addEventListener("abort", () => resolve(), { once: true });
				});
				context.abortSignal?.throwIfAborted();
				throw new Error("The code mode sandbox was closed while the script ran");
			}
			// A replay that ends where the live run went on is a divergence (design §4.3).
			const during = result.during;
			// Within the replayed part, the first run included: a rerun without journal entries replays that much too.
			if (this.rerun && during !== undefined && during.seq <= this.replayed.length) {
				// The live run went on: it acknowledged that result, or it made a call that later results answer, which the
				// replay never made.
				const continued =
					(during.seq === 0
						? this.replayed.length > 0 || this.firstIssued !== undefined
						: this.replayed[during.seq - 1]?.issuedAfter !== undefined) ||
					this.replayed.slice(during.seq).some((entry) => entry.id > during.issued);
				if (continued) {
					return this.decide(
						errorResult(
							`The script took a different path when resumed after a restart: it ended after result ${during.seq}, where it went on before.`,
						),
					);
				}
			}
			return this.complete(result);
		} finally {
			context.abortSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
		}
	}

	/** Store an outcome the call decided, and end the VM if it runs; returns the outcome. */
	        async decide(decided                     )                               {
		const result = { ...decided, details: this.details()                         };
		await this.api.commit(async (tx) => {
			const run = await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, this.api.taskId);
			run.result ??= copyJson(result                        , { omitUndefinedProperties: true })              ;
		}, this.context);
		return result;
	}

	/** End the call with `message`: store it as the outcome, then stop the VM with an error the script cannot catch. */
	        diverge(message        )       {
		if (this.ended !== undefined || this.failure !== undefined) return;
		const result = errorResult(`The script took a different path when resumed after a restart: ${message}`);
		this.ended = result;
		this.decide(result).then(
			() => this.controller.abort(new Error(message)),
			(error         ) => this.fail(error),
		);
	}

	/** Whether nothing more may happen: the run has its outcome, or the call decided or failed. */
	        get stopped()          {
		return this.ended !== undefined || this.failure !== undefined || this.run?.finished === true;
	}

	        fail(error         )       {
		if (this.failure !== undefined) return;
		this.failure = error;
		this.controller.abort(error);
	}

	/** The script's own outcome, stored with its store writes in one transaction. */
	        async complete(result                )                               {
		const details = this.details();
		const formatted = await formatResult(result, {
			generatedImages: this.generatedImages,
			wallMs: Date.now() - this.start.startedAt,
			maxOutputTokens: this.start.maxOutputTokens,
			calls: details.calls,
			limits: OUTPUT_LIMITS,
			write: this.outputFiles(),
		});
		const tool                      = {
			output: formatted.content,
			isError: !result.ok,
			details: {
				...details,
				...(formatted.fullOutputPath === undefined ? {} : { fullOutputPath: formatted.fullOutputPath }),
			}                        ,
		};
		const writes = result.ok ? result.storeWrites : undefined;
		return this.api.commit(async (tx) => {
			const run = await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, this.api.taskId);
			if (run.result !== undefined) return run.result                       ;
			run.result = copyJson(tool                        , { omitUndefinedProperties: true })              ;
			if (writes !== undefined && (Object.keys(writes.set).length > 0 || writes.delete.length > 0)) {
				const store = await tx.doc(CodemodeStoreDoc, this.api.conversationId);
				for (const key of writes.delete) delete store.values[key];
				for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(writes.set)) store.values[key] = value             ;
			}
			return tool;
		}, this.context);
	}

	/** Writes the result's files under fixed names into the run's output directory, so a rerun overwrites them. */
	        outputFiles()             {
		const env = this.api.env;
		let directory                             ;
		return async (name, data) => {
			if (env === undefined) return { error: "no execution environment" };
			try {
				directory ??= this.outputDir(env);
				const path = `${await directory}/${name}`;
				const written = await env.writeFile(path, data, this.context);
				return written.ok ? { path } : { error: written.error.message };
			} catch (error) {
				return { error: errorMessage(error) };
			}
		};
	}

	/** The run's output directory: the recorded one, else a new temp directory, recorded before any file is written. */
	        async outputDir(env              )                  {
		const recorded = await this.api.commit(
			async (tx) => (await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, this.api.taskId)).outputDir,
			this.context,
		);
		if (recorded !== undefined) return recorded;
		const created = await env.createTempDir("pi-codemode-", this.context);
		if (!created.ok) throw new Error(created.error.message);
		return this.api.commit(async (tx) => {
			const run = await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, this.api.taskId);
			run.outputDir ??= created.value;
			return run.outputDir ;
		}, this.context);
	}

	        async checkFingerprint(fingerprint        )                {
		const recorded = this.start.fingerprint;
		if (recorded !== undefined) {
			if (recorded !== fingerprint) {
				this.ended = errorResult(
					`The script cannot resume: the sandbox changed since it started (${recorded} -> ${fingerprint}).`,
				);
				await this.decide(this.ended).catch((error         ) => this.fail(error));
				this.controller.abort(new Error("Sandbox fingerprint changed"));
				return;
			}
		} else {
			try {
				await this.api.commit(async (tx) => {
					const run = await tx.doc(CodemodeRunDoc, this.api.taskId);
					if (run.start !== undefined) run.start.fingerprint = fingerprint;
				}, this.context);
			} catch (error) {
				this.fail(error);
				return;
			}
		}
		this.openGate();
		// Nothing was delivered before the gate: start now. A rerun without a journal releases its calls at the
		// script's first acknowledgment instead.
		if (this.replaying) this.deliverReplayed();
		else if (!this.rerun) void this.release();
	}

	        onCall(made                  )       {
		if (this.stopped) return;
		const call = asToolCall(made);
		if (call.target === "tool") this.started(call);
		this.issued = Math.max(this.issued, call.id);
		const index = this.replaying ? (this.replayIndex.get(call.id) ?? -1) : -1;
		if (index === -1) {
			if (this.released) {
				this.waiting.push(call);
				this.launchCovered();
			} else this.held.push(call);
			return;
		}
		const entry = this.replayed[index] ;
		if (entry.global !== undefined) {
			if (call.target !== "global" || call.name !== entry.global.name || !sameJson(call.args, entry.global.args)) {
				this.diverge(`call ${call.id} is ${describe(call)}, but was a call to ${entry.global.name}`);
				return;
			}
			this.ready.set(call.id, entry.global.outcome                   );
			void this.gate.then(() => this.deliverReplayed());
			return;
		}
		if (call.target !== "tool") {
			this.diverge(`call ${call.id} is ${describe(call)}, but was a tool call`);
			return;
		}
		void this.gate
			.then(() => this.callTool(call))
			.then(
				(outcome) => {
					this.ready.set(call.id, outcome);
					this.deliverReplayed();
				},
				(error         ) => {
					if (this.stopped) return;
					if (this.context.abortSignal?.aborted) this.fail(error);
					// The key names another tool or other arguments.
					else this.diverge(`call ${call.id} is ${describe(call)}: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
				},
			);
	}

	/** Replay: deliver journaled results in journal order, each once the VM has issued its call. */
	        deliverReplayed()       {
		while (this.nextReplayed < this.replayed.length && !this.stopped) {
			const entry = this.replayed[this.nextReplayed] ;
			const outcome = this.ready.get(entry.id);
			if (entry.id > this.issued || outcome === undefined) return;
			this.ready.delete(entry.id);
			this.nextReplayed++;
			this.run.deliver(this.nextReplayed, entry.at, entry.id, outcome);
		}
	}

	        onIdle(seq        , issued        )       {
		if (this.stopped) return;
		// A count an earlier attempt recorded must match; a missing one is recorded now.
		if (this.rerun && seq <= this.replayed.length) {
			const recorded = seq === 0 ? this.firstIssued : this.replayed[seq - 1] .issuedAfter;
			if (recorded !== undefined && recorded !== issued) {
				this.diverge(
					seq === 0
						? `its first run made ${issued} calls, where it had made ${recorded}`
						: `after result ${seq} it had made ${issued} calls, where it had made ${recorded}`,
				);
				return;
			}
		}
		this.acks.set(seq, issued);
		if (!this.replaying) {
			// A rerun without a journal: every call an earlier attempt could have admitted is made by now.
			if (seq === 0 && this.rerun) void this.release();
			// Committed at once when the script has processed everything delivered: calls it made wait for it, and a script
			// waiting on a long call is where a restart usually finds it. Otherwise with the next commit; the VM catches up
			// without waiting on anything.
			if (seq === this.journalLength) void this.pump();
			return;
		}
		// Caught up: the VM processed everything delivered and waits.
		if (seq !== this.nextReplayed) return;
		const next = this.replayed[seq];
		if (next !== undefined) {
			if (next.id > issued) this.diverge(`it waits without making call ${next.id}, which it made before`);
			return;
		}
		// The last replayed result is acknowledged: continue live, admitting the calls the journal did not have once their
		// count is committed.
		this.replaying = false;
		void this.pump();
		void this.release();
	}

	/**
	 * Let the held calls launch, and later live calls, each once its issued count is committed. On a rerun, an earlier
	 * attempt may have admitted some of them under their keys, as other calls: when the replay releases (idle after the
	 * last journaled result, or after its first run without a journal) its counts matched every recorded one, so it has
	 * made every call an earlier attempt could have admitted. All held calls are checked first, whatever their target,
	 * and a divergence launches none. Calls made after this are new.
	 */
	        async release()                {
		await this.gate;
		if (this.stopped || this.released) return;
		const calls = this.held.splice(0);
		if (this.rerun) {
			for (const call of calls) {
				let other                    ;
				try {
					other = await this.earlier(call);
				} catch (error) {
					this.fail(error);
					return;
				}
				if (this.stopped) return;
				if (other !== undefined) {
					this.diverge(`call ${call.id} is ${describe(call)}, but was ${other}`);
					return;
				}
			}
		}
		this.released = true;
		this.waiting.push(...calls, ...this.held.splice(0));
		this.launchCovered();
	}

	/** Launch the waiting calls whose issued count is committed. */
	        launchCovered()       {
		if (!this.released || this.stopped) return;
		const covered = this.waiting.filter((call) => call.id <= this.durableIssued);
		if (covered.length === 0) return;
		this.waiting = this.waiting.filter((call) => call.id > this.durableIssued);
		for (const call of covered) this.launch(call);
	}

	/** The call an earlier attempt made under this call's key, when it is another one: another target, tool, or arguments. */
	        async earlier(call                  )                              {
		const { api, context } = this;
		const taskId = (await api.snapshot(NestedCallDoc, api.taskId, `c${call.id}`, context))?.taskId;
		if (taskId === undefined) return undefined;
		const input = (await api.getTask(taskId, context))?.input                             ;
		if (input?.kind !== "nested") return undefined;
		const made = input.call;
		if (call.target === "tool" && made.name === call.name && sameJson(made.arguments, call.args ?? {}))
			return undefined;
		return `tools.${made.name}(${JSON.stringify(made.arguments)})`;
	}

	/** Run one live call and journal its result. */
	        launch(call                  )       {
		if (this.stopped) return;
		const running                                                                         =
			call.target === "tool"
				? this.callTool(call).then((outcome) => ({ outcome }))
				: this.callGlobal(call).then((outcome) => ({
						outcome,
						global: {
							name: call.name,
							...(call.args === undefined ? {} : { args: call.args              }),
							outcome: outcome              ,
						},
					}));
		running.then(
			(done) => {
				this.queue.push({ id: call.id, at: Date.now(), ...done });
				void this.pump();
			},
			(error         ) => {
				// Stopped meanwhile: the outcome is decided, or a waiting model call gave up.
				if (this.stopped) return;
				if (this.context.abortSignal?.aborted) this.fail(error);
				// A key an earlier attempt admitted with another tool or other arguments: this attempt took another path.
				else this.diverge(`call ${call.id} is ${describe(call)}: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
			},
		);
	}

	        async callTool(call                  )                           {
		// Passed as the script gave them; validation reports arguments that are not an object.
		const args = (call.args ?? {})              ;
		const model = call.name.startsWith(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX);
		if (model) {
			await this.modelSlot();
			// The call may have stopped while this one waited: a finished script admits no new paid call.
			if (this.stopped) {
				this.releaseModelSlot();
				throw new Error("The script has ended");
			}
		}
		let result                           ;
		try {
			result = await this.api.executeTool(call.name, args, this.context, { key: `c${call.id}` });
		} finally {
			if (model) this.releaseModelSlot();
		}
		if (call.name === GENERATE_IMAGES_TOOL) this.generatedImages += countImages(result.structuredOutput);
		const structured = this.start.tools.find((tool) => tool.name === call.name)?.structured === true;
		const outcome = outcomeOf(call.name, structured, result);
		this.finishRow(call.id, outcome, result);
		return outcome;
	}

	/** The script made a tool call: its row appears, running, also while it waits to launch. */
	        started(call                  )       {
		if (this.rows.has(call.id)) return;
		const args = call.args === undefined ? "" : (JSON.stringify(call.args) ?? "");
		const name = call.name.startsWith(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX)
			? call.name.slice(CODEMODE_INTERNAL_PREFIX.length)
			: call.name;
		this.rows.set(call.id, { id: call.id, name, args: cut(args, ARGS_PREVIEW_CHARS), status: "running" });
	}

	/** A tool call ended: its row gets its status, duration, and error. */
	/** The call ended: an error result is an error row, also when the script got its value (bash's nonzero exit). */
	        finishRow(id        , outcome                 , result                           )       {
		const row = this.rows.get(id);
		if (row === undefined) return;
		row.status = result.isError ? "error" : "ok";
		if (result.durationMs !== undefined) row.durationMs = result.durationMs;
		const errors = result.diagnostics.filter((diagnostic) => diagnostic.severity === "error");
		const error = !outcome.ok ? outcome.message : errors.map((diagnostic) => diagnostic.message).join("\n");
		if (result.isError && error !== "") row.error = cut(error, ERROR_PREVIEW_CHARS);
	}

	/** The rows in call order; calls still running when the script ended were cancelled. */
	        details()                      {
		const calls = [...this.rows.values()]
			.sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
			.map((row) => (row.status === "running" ? { ...row, status: "cancelled"          } : row));
		return { calls };
	}

	        async callGlobal(call                  )                           {
		const discovery = (DISCOVERY_GLOBALS                     ).includes(call.name);
		const catalog = (CATALOG_GLOBALS                     ).includes(call.name);
		const global =
			discovery || catalog ? undefined : this.options.globals?.find((candidate) => candidate.name === call.name);
		if (!discovery && !catalog && global === undefined) return { ok: false, message: `Unknown global ${call.name}` };
		try {
			// `undefined` stays undefined: the journal omits the value, and a replay resolves with undefined again.
			const value = discovery
				? this.discover(call)
				: catalog
					? await catalogCall(call.name, call.args, this.api.models, this.context)
					: await global .execute(call.args, this.api, this.context);
			return { ok: true, value };
		} catch (error) {
			if (this.context.abortSignal?.aborted) throw error;
			return { ok: false, message: errorMessage(error) };
		}
	}

	        async modelSlot()                {
		if (this.modelCalls >= MAX_CONCURRENT_MODEL_CALLS) {
			await new Promise      ((resolve) => this.modelWaiters.push(resolve));
		}
		this.modelCalls++;
	}

	        releaseModelSlot()       {
		this.modelCalls--;
		this.modelWaiters.shift()?.();
	}

	/** `searchTools()`, `describeTool()`, `describeNamespace()`: answered from the start data. */
	        discover(call                  )                        {
		const args = Array.isArray(call.args) ? call.args : [];
		const { namespaces } = this.start;
		const tools = this.start.tools.filter((tool) => tool.hidden !== true);
		const namespaceOfTool = (name        ) => namespaces.find((namespace) => namespace.tools.includes(name));
		if (call.name === "describeTool") {
			const [name] = args;
			if (typeof name !== "string") throw new Error("describeTool() expects a tool name");
			return tools.find((tool) => tool.name === name || tool.jsName === name)?.description;
		}
		if (call.name === "describeNamespace") {
			const [name] = args;
			if (typeof name !== "string") throw new Error("describeNamespace() expects a namespace name");
			const namespace = namespaces.find((candidate) => matchesNamespace(candidate, name));
			if (namespace === undefined) return undefined;
			return {
				name: namespace.name,
				...(namespace.description === undefined ? {} : { description: namespace.description }),
				...(namespace.instructions === undefined ? {} : { instructions: namespace.instructions }),
				tools: namespace.tools.map((tool) => toCodemodeIdentifier(tool)),
			};
		}
		const [query, options] = args                                                                   ;
		if (typeof query !== "string") throw new Error("searchTools() expects a query string");
		const limit = options?.limit ?? DEFAULT_SEARCH_LIMIT;
		if (typeof limit !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit <= 0) {
			throw new Error("searchTools() limit must be a positive integer");
		}
		const namespace = options?.namespace;
		if (namespace !== undefined && namespace !== null && typeof namespace !== "string") {
			throw new Error("searchTools() namespace must be a string");
		}
		const documents = tools.flatMap((tool) => {
			const own = namespaceOfTool(tool.name);
			if (typeof namespace === "string" && (own === undefined || !matchesNamespace(own, namespace))) return [];
			const text = [
				tool.name,
				tool.name.replaceAll("_", " "),
				tool.description,
				own?.name ?? "",
				own?.description ?? "",
			];
			return [{ name: tool.name, text: text.join(" ") }];
		});
		return rankDocuments(query, documents, limit).map((name) => {
			const tool = tools.find((candidate) => candidate.name === name) ;
			return { name: tool.jsName, description: tool.description };
		});
	}

	/**
	 * Journal finished calls and acknowledged counts in one commit, then deliver the calls and launch the calls the
	 * counts cover; one commit in flight at a time, so what arrives meanwhile goes into the next.
	 */
	        async pump()                {
		if (this.committing || this.stopped) return;
		if (this.queue.length === 0 && this.acks.size === 0) return;
		this.committing = true;
		const batch = this.queue.splice(0);
		const acks = [...this.acks];
		this.acks.clear();
		try {
			await this.api.commit(async (tx) => {
				const journal = await tx.doc(CodemodeJournalDoc, this.api.taskId);
				for (const [seq, issued] of acks) {
					if (seq === 0) journal.firstIssued ??= issued;
					else {
						const entry = journal.entries[seq - 1];
						if (entry !== undefined && entry.issuedAfter === undefined) entry.issuedAfter = issued;
					}
				}
				for (const item of batch) {
					const entry = {
						id: item.id,
						at: item.at,
						...(item.global === undefined ? {} : { global: item.global }),
					};
					journal.entries.push(
						copyJson(entry                        , { omitUndefinedProperties: true })                           ,
					);
				}
			}, this.context);
		} catch (error) {
			this.fail(error);
			return;
		}
		for (const [, issued] of acks) this.durableIssued = Math.max(this.durableIssued, issued);
		for (const item of batch) {
			this.journalLength++;
			if (!this.run.finished) this.run.deliver(this.journalLength, item.at, item.id, item.outcome);
		}
		this.launchCovered();
		this.committing = false;
		void this.pump();
	}
}

/** A script's call of `models.classify()` or `models.generateImages()` as the call of its internal tool. */
function asToolCall(call                  )                   {
	const tool = call.target === "global" ? MODEL_CALLS[call.name] : undefined;
	if (tool === undefined) return call;
	const [model, context] = Array.isArray(call.args) ? call.args : [];
	return { id: call.id, target: "tool", name: tool, args: { model: model ?? null, context: context ?? null } };
}

/** Image blocks in a `models.generateImages()` result. */
function countImages(value         )         {
	const output = typeof value === "object" && value !== null ? (value                        ).output : undefined;
	return Array.isArray(output) ? output.filter((item) => (item                      )?.type === "image").length : 0;
}

function describe(call                  )         {
	return `${call.target === "tool" ? "tools." : ""}${call.name}(${JSON.stringify(call.args) ?? ""})`;
}

/** Structural JSON equality; key order does not matter. */
function sameJson(a         , b         )          {
	if (a === b) return true;
	if (typeof a !== "object" || typeof b !== "object" || a === null || b === null) return false;
	if (Array.isArray(a) || Array.isArray(b)) {
		return (
			Array.isArray(a) && Array.isArray(b) && a.length === b.length && a.every((item, i) => sameJson(item, b[i]))
		);
	}
	const keys = Object.keys(a);
	return (
		keys.length === Object.keys(b).length &&
		keys.every(
			(key) =>
				Object.hasOwn(b, key) && sameJson((a                           )[key], (b                           )[key]),
		)
	);
}

// ══ src/codemode/index.ts ══


// ══ src/storage/scan.ts ══
                                                   
                                                 

/** Where a built-in storage scan starts: its order and the last ID a previous page returned. */
                         
	                          
	                                   
  

/**
 * Resolve a scan's order and position. A cursor continues in the order it was created with, whether the query repeats
 * that order or omits it; a different `order` throws. A cursor without an order, written before scans had one,
 * continues in the scan's default order.
 */
       function scanStart(
	requested                       ,
	cursor                                               ,
	fallback           ,
)            {
	if (requested !== undefined && requested !== "ascending" && requested !== "descending") {
		throw new StorageRequestError(`Invalid scan order: ${String(requested)}`);
	}
	if (cursor === undefined) return { order: requested ?? fallback, after: undefined };
	const { after, order: stored } = cursor;
	if (typeof after !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(after))
		throw new StorageRequestError("Invalid storage cursor");
	if (stored !== undefined && stored !== "ascending" && stored !== "descending") {
		throw new StorageRequestError("Invalid storage cursor");
	}
	const order = stored ?? fallback;
	if (requested !== undefined && requested !== order) {
		throw new StorageRequestError(`The cursor continues a ${order} scan; the query asks for ${requested}`);
	}
	return { order, after };
}

/** The continuation of a scan whose last returned item has `id`. */
       function nextCursor(id            , order           )                                                        {
	return { after: id, order };
}

// ══ src/storage/memory.ts ══
                                                                
                                                                             
                                                   
                                                        
             
                
                   
                    
        
                 
                 
                
            
               
               
                
         
            
             
    
            
      
           
     
         
              
                
              
                 
                  
        
           
            
                     
                                                                  

                                                              
                                                
                                                   
                                                                               
                                                                
                            
	                       
	                              
  
                       
	                        
	                          
	                
  

function* documentDeltaBatches(
	id            ,
	version        ,
	revisions                             ,
	start        ,
)                           {
	for (let index = start; index < revisions.length; index++) {
		const revision = revisions[index] ;
		if (revision.kind !== "delta" || revision.version !== version) {
			throw new Error(`Document ${id} crosses a stored version boundary without a base`);
		}
		yield revision.ops;
	}
}

                             
	                  
	                       
  

              
	                                        
	                                                       
	                                  
	                                                                          
	                                                          
	                                   
	                                         
	                                             
	                                   
	                               
	                  
	                                              
	                                                 
	                              
	                                                                
	                                                                       
	                                                
	                                                     
	                                              
  

const clone =    (value   )    => {
	if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return value;
	if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => clone(item))     ;
	const source = value                           ;
	const nullPrototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value) === null;
	const result = (nullPrototype ? Object.create(null) : {})                           ;
	for (const key of Object.keys(source)) {
		const copied = clone(source[key]);
		if (!nullPrototype && key in result) {
			Object.defineProperty(result, key, {
				value: copied,
				writable: true,
				enumerable: true,
				configurable: true,
			});
		} else {
			result[key] = copied;
		}
	}
	return result     ;
};

const freeze =    (value   )    => {
	if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || Object.isFrozen(value)) return value;
	for (const child of Object.values(value)) freeze(child);
	return Object.freeze(value);
};

const cursorId =                       (cursor                                                 )                => {
	const after = cursor?.after;
	if (after === undefined) return undefined;
	if (typeof after !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(after))
		throw new StorageRequestError("Invalid storage cursor");
	return idFromNumber   (after);
};

const lowerBound =                       (ids              , target        )         => {
	let low = 0;
	let high = ids.length;
	while (low < high) {
		const middle = (low + high) >>> 1;
		if (ids[middle] < target) low = middle + 1;
		else high = middle;
	}
	return low;
};

const upperBound =                       (ids              , target        )         => {
	let low = 0;
	let high = ids.length;
	while (low < high) {
		const middle = (low + high) >>> 1;
		if (ids[middle] <= target) low = middle + 1;
		else high = middle;
	}
	return low;
};

const insertSorted =                       (ids     , id   )       => {
	if (ids.length === 0 || ids[ids.length - 1] < id) ids.push(id);
	else ids.splice(lowerBound(ids, id), 0, id);
};

const removeSorted =                       (ids     , id   )       => {
	const index = lowerBound(ids, id);
	if (ids[index] === id) ids.splice(index, 1);
};

const insertMapId =                          (index             , key   , id   )       => {
	let ids = index.get(key);
	if (ids === undefined) {
		ids = [];
		index.set(key, ids);
	}
	insertSorted(ids, id);
};

const scopeKey = (scope                         )         => {
	switch (scope.kind) {
		case "session":
			return JSON.stringify(["session"]);
		case "conversation":
			return JSON.stringify(["conversation", scope.conversationId]);
		case "task":
			return JSON.stringify(["task", scope.taskId]);
	}
};

const addressKey = (address                 )         =>
	JSON.stringify([
		address.kind,
		scopeKey(address.scope),
		address.key === undefined ? ["singleton"] : ["family", address.key],
	]);

const recordAddressKey = (record                                 )         =>
	addressKey({ kind: record.kind, scope: record.scope, key: record.key });

const isAliveAt = (record                , at               )          => {
	if (at === "current") return record.retiredAt === undefined;
	return record.createdAt <= at && (record.retiredAt === undefined || at < record.retiredAt);
};

const isCurrentOnly = (record                )          =>
	record.scope.kind !== "conversation" || record.history === "latest";

const tableContaining = (state       , id            )                        => state.recordTypes.get(id);

const page =                                        (
	values              ,
	limit        ,
	order            = "ascending",
)                  => {
	const items = values.slice(0, limit);
	if (values.length <= limit) return { items: clone(items) };
	return { items: clone(items), next: nextCursor(items.at(-1) .id, order) };
};

/** Indexes of sorted `ids` in scan order, after the cursor's ID when there is one. */
function* scanIndexes(ids                       , start           )                    {
	if (start.order === "ascending") {
		const first = start.after === undefined ? 0 : upperBound(ids, start.after);
		for (let index = first; index < ids.length; index++) yield index;
	} else {
		const end = start.after === undefined ? ids.length : lowerBound(ids, start.after);
		for (let index = end - 1; index >= 0; index--) yield index;
	}
}

/** A fully validated, detached state mutation whose application performs no fallible preparation. */
                                       
	                  
	                                                     
	                                         
	             
 

/**
 * Detached in-memory reference implementation of `Storage`.
 *
 * Reads and retained writes are cloned intentionally to match the ownership boundary
 * of serialization-backed stores. This is backend conformance, not validation.
 */
       class MemoryStorage                    {
	                 state        = {
		recordTypes: new Map(),
		conversations: new Map(),
		conversationIds: [],
		conversationIdsByOwnerConversation: new Map(),
		conversationIdsByOwnerTask: new Map(),
		entries: new Map(),
		entryIds: new Map(),
		headEntryIds: new Map(),
		entryCommitSeqs: new Map(),
		tasks: new Map(),
		taskIds: [],
		taskIdsByStatus: { pending: [], running: [], waiting: [], completing: [], terminal: [] },
		submissions: new Map(),
		submissionIds: [],
		submissionIdsByStatus: { queued: [], placed: [], done: [], unanswered: [] },
		submissionIdsByRequest: new Map(),
		documents: new Map(),
		documentAddresses: new Map(),
		documentIdsByScope: new Map(),
	};
	        nextId = 2;
	        nextSeq = 1;
	        closed = false;

	async commit(writes                         , _context         )               {
		return this.prepareCommit(writes).apply();
	}

	/** Validate and detach one commit without changing observable state. */
	prepareCommit(writes                         , seq      = seqFromNumber(this.nextSeq))                       {
		this.assertOpen();
		if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seq) || seq < this.nextSeq) {
			throw new Error(`Commit sequence ${seq} does not strictly increase`);
		}
		const detachedWrites = freeze(this.resolveDocumentCopies(writes.map((write) => clone(write))));
		this.checkGlobalIds(detachedWrites);
		const documentActions = this.prepareDocumentActions(detachedWrites);
		this.checkDocumentActions(documentActions);
		let applied = false;
		return {
			seq,
			writes: detachedWrites,
			apply: () => {
				if (!applied) {
					applied = true;
					this.applyPreparedCommit(detachedWrites, documentActions, seq);
				}
				return seq;
			},
		};
	}

	        resolveDocumentCopies(writes                )                 {
		if (!writes.some((write) => write.type === "document.copy")) return writes;
		const changedDocumentIds = new Set            ();
		for (const write of writes) {
			if (write.type === "document.create" || write.type === "document.copy") {
				changedDocumentIds.add(write.record.id);
			} else if (write.type === "document.change" || write.type === "document.retire") {
				changedDocumentIds.add(write.id);
			}
		}
		return writes.map((write) => {
			if (write.type !== "document.copy") return write;
			try {
				if (changedDocumentIds.has(write.source.id)) {
					throw new Error(`Fork source document ${write.source.id} is changed in the copy batch`);
				}
				const stored = this.materializeDocument(write.source.id, write.source.at);
				if (stored === undefined) throw new Error(`Fork source document ${write.source.id} cannot be read`);
				if (
					stored.record.scope.kind !== "conversation" ||
					write.record.scope.kind !== "conversation" ||
					stored.record.kind !== write.record.kind ||
					stored.record.key !== write.record.key ||
					stored.record.history !== write.record.history ||
					stored.record.fork !== write.record.fork
				) {
					throw new Error(`Fork source document ${write.source.id} does not match the copied record`);
				}
				return {
					type: "document.create",
					record: write.record,
					content: { kind: "base", version: stored.version, value: stored.value },
				};
			} catch (error) {
				throw new Error(`Document copy ${write.record.id} was rejected`, { cause: error });
			}
		});
	}

	        applyPreparedCommit(
		prepared                         ,
		documentActions                                         ,
		seq     ,
	)      {
		for (const write of prepared) {
			switch (write.type) {
				case "conversation": {
					this.state.recordTypes.set(write.value.id, "conversation");
					this.state.conversations.set(write.value.id, write.value);
					insertSorted(this.state.conversationIds, write.value.id);
					const owner = write.value.owner;
					if (owner !== undefined) {
						insertMapId(this.state.conversationIdsByOwnerConversation, owner.conversationId, write.value.id);
						insertMapId(this.state.conversationIdsByOwnerTask, owner.taskId, write.value.id);
					}
					this.nextId = Math.max(this.nextId, write.value.id + 1);
					break;
				}
				case "entry": {
					this.state.recordTypes.set(write.value.id, "entry");
					this.state.entries.set(write.value.id, write.value);
					this.state.entryCommitSeqs.set(write.value.id, seq);
					let ids = this.state.entryIds.get(write.value.conversationId);
					if (ids === undefined) {
						ids = [];
						this.state.entryIds.set(write.value.conversationId, ids);
					}
					insertSorted(ids, write.value.id);
					if (write.value.head !== undefined) {
						let headIds = this.state.headEntryIds.get(write.value.conversationId);
						if (headIds === undefined) {
							headIds = [];
							this.state.headEntryIds.set(write.value.conversationId, headIds);
						}
						insertSorted(headIds, write.value.id);
					}
					this.nextId = Math.max(this.nextId, write.value.id + 1);
					break;
				}
				case "task": {
					this.state.recordTypes.set(write.value.id, "task");
					const previous = this.state.tasks.get(write.value.id);
					if (previous === undefined) {
						insertSorted(this.state.taskIds, write.value.id);
						insertSorted(this.state.taskIdsByStatus[write.value.state.status], write.value.id);
					} else if (previous.state.status !== write.value.state.status) {
						removeSorted(this.state.taskIdsByStatus[previous.state.status], write.value.id);
						insertSorted(this.state.taskIdsByStatus[write.value.state.status], write.value.id);
					}
					this.state.tasks.set(write.value.id, write.value);
					this.nextId = Math.max(this.nextId, write.value.id + 1);
					break;
				}
				case "submission": {
					this.state.recordTypes.set(write.value.id, "submission");
					const previous = this.state.submissions.get(write.value.id);
					if (previous === undefined) {
						insertSorted(this.state.submissionIds, write.value.id);
						insertSorted(this.state.submissionIdsByStatus[write.value.status], write.value.id);
					} else if (previous.status !== write.value.status) {
						removeSorted(this.state.submissionIdsByStatus[previous.status], write.value.id);
						insertSorted(this.state.submissionIdsByStatus[write.value.status], write.value.id);
					}
					if (previous?.requestId !== undefined) {
						const previousRequests = this.state.submissionIdsByRequest.get(previous.conversationId);
						if (previousRequests?.get(previous.requestId) === write.value.id) {
							previousRequests.delete(previous.requestId);
							if (previousRequests.size === 0) this.state.submissionIdsByRequest.delete(previous.conversationId);
						}
					}
					this.state.submissions.set(write.value.id, write.value);
					if (write.value.requestId !== undefined) {
						let requests = this.state.submissionIdsByRequest.get(write.value.conversationId);
						if (requests === undefined) {
							requests = new Map();
							this.state.submissionIdsByRequest.set(write.value.conversationId, requests);
						}
						requests.set(write.value.requestId, write.value.id);
					}
					this.nextId = Math.max(this.nextId, write.value.id + 1);
					break;
				}
				case "document.copy":
					throw new Error("Prepared document copy was not resolved");
				case "document.create":
				case "document.change":
				case "document.retire":
					break;
			}
		}

		this.applyDocumentActions(documentActions, seq);
		this.nextSeq = seq + 1;
		return seq;
	}

	async mintId                      ()             {
		this.assertOpen();
		if (!Number.isSafeInteger(this.nextId)) throw new Error("ID space is exhausted");
		return idFromNumber   (this.nextId++);
	}

	async conversation(id                , _context         )                                          {
		this.assertOpen();
		const value = this.state.conversations.get(id);
		return value === undefined ? undefined : clone(value);
	}

	async scanConversations(
		query                   ,
		limit        ,
		cursor                    ,
		_context         ,
	)                                            {
		this.assertOpen();
		const ids =
			query.ownerTaskId !== undefined
				? (this.state.conversationIdsByOwnerTask.get(query.ownerTaskId) ?? [])
				: query.ownerConversationId !== undefined
					? (this.state.conversationIdsByOwnerConversation.get(query.ownerConversationId) ?? [])
					: this.state.conversationIds;
		const start = scanStart(query.order, cursor, "ascending");
		const values                       = [];
		for (const index of scanIndexes(ids, start)) {
			if (values.length > limit) break;
			const value = this.state.conversations.get(ids[index] ) ;
			if (query.ownerConversationId !== undefined && value.owner?.conversationId !== query.ownerConversationId) {
				continue;
			}
			values.push(value);
		}
		return page(values, limit, start.order);
	}

	                                                                                                                    
	      
		                               
		            
		                 
	                                                                                 
	async entry(
		idOrConversationId                          ,
		idOrContext                   ,
		context          ,
	)                                                                                {
		this.assertOpen();
		if (context === undefined) {
			const id = idFromNumber         (idOrConversationId);
			const entry = this.state.entries.get(id);
			if (entry === undefined) return undefined;
			return { entry: clone(entry), commitSeq: this.state.entryCommitSeqs.get(id)  };
		}
		if (typeof idOrContext !== "number") throw new StorageRequestError("Storage.entry() requires an entry ID");
		const conversationId = idFromNumber                (idOrConversationId);
		const id = idFromNumber         (idOrContext);
		const entry = this.visibleEntries(conversationId, id, id).next().value;
		if (entry === undefined) return undefined;
		return { entry: clone(entry), commitSeq: this.state.entryCommitSeqs.get(id)  };
	}

	async findLatestHeadMarker(
		conversationId                ,
		atOrBeforeEntryId                     ,
		_context         ,
	)                                                                  {
		this.assertOpen();
		if (!this.state.conversations.has(conversationId)) {
			throw new StorageRequestError(`Unknown conversation: ${conversationId}`);
		}
		let currentId = conversationId;
		let upperEntryId = atOrBeforeEntryId ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		while (true) {
			const ids = this.state.headEntryIds.get(currentId) ?? [];
			const index = upperBound(ids, upperEntryId) - 1;
			if (index >= 0) {
				const entry = this.state.entries.get(ids[index]) ;
				return clone({ ...entry, head: entry.head  });
			}
			const conversation = this.state.conversations.get(currentId) ;
			if (conversation.parent === undefined) return undefined;
			upperEntryId = Math.min(upperEntryId, conversation.parent.at);
			currentId = conversation.parent.conversationId;
		}
	}

	async scanEntries(
		query            ,
		limit        ,
		cursor                    ,
		_context         ,
	)                                     {
		this.assertOpen();
		const { order, after } = scanStart(query.order, cursor, "descending");
		// The cursor narrows the bound on the side the scan moves away from.
		let minEntryId                     = query.minEntryId;
		let maxEntryId                     = query.maxEntryId;
		if (after !== undefined && order === "descending") maxEntryId = Math.min(maxEntryId ?? after, after - 1);
		if (after !== undefined && order === "ascending") minEntryId = Math.max(minEntryId ?? after, after + 1);
		const visible                = [];
		const entries =
			order === "descending"
				? this.visibleEntries(query.conversationId, minEntryId, maxEntryId)
				: this.visibleEntriesAscending(query.conversationId, minEntryId, maxEntryId);
		for (const entry of entries) {
			visible.push(entry);
			if (visible.length > limit) break;
		}
		return page(visible, limit, order);
	}

	async task(id        , _context         )                                  {
		this.assertOpen();
		const value = this.state.tasks.get(id);
		return value === undefined ? undefined : clone(value);
	}

	async scanTasks(
		query           ,
		limit        ,
		cursor                    ,
		_context         ,
	)                                    {
		this.assertOpen();
		const start = scanStart(query.order, cursor, "ascending");
		const ids = query.status === undefined ? this.state.taskIds : this.state.taskIdsByStatus[query.status];
		const values               = [];
		for (const index of scanIndexes(ids, start)) {
			if (values.length > limit) break;
			const value = this.state.tasks.get(ids[index] ) ;
			if (query.conversationId !== undefined && value.conversationId !== query.conversationId) continue;
			if (query.kind !== undefined && value.kind !== query.kind) continue;
			if (query.abortRequested !== undefined && value.abortRequested !== query.abortRequested) continue;
			if (query.background !== undefined && value.background !== query.background) continue;
			values.push(value);
		}
		return page(values, limit, start.order);
	}

	async submission(id              , _context         )                                        {
		this.assertOpen();
		const value = this.state.submissions.get(id);
		return value === undefined ? undefined : clone(value);
	}

	async scanSubmissions(
		query                 ,
		limit        ,
		cursor                    ,
		_context         ,
	)                                          {
		this.assertOpen();
		const start = scanStart(query.order, cursor, "ascending");
		const ids =
			query.status === undefined ? this.state.submissionIds : this.state.submissionIdsByStatus[query.status];
		const values                     = [];
		for (const index of scanIndexes(ids, start)) {
			if (values.length > limit) break;
			const value = this.state.submissions.get(ids[index] ) ;
			if (query.conversationId !== undefined && value.conversationId !== query.conversationId) continue;
			values.push(value);
		}
		return page(values, limit, start.order);
	}

	async submissionByRequest(
		conversationId                ,
		requestId        ,
		_context         ,
	)                                        {
		this.assertOpen();
		const id = this.state.submissionIdsByRequest.get(conversationId)?.get(requestId);
		if (id === undefined) return undefined;
		return clone(this.state.submissions.get(id) );
	}

	async findDocument(
		address                 ,
		at               ,
		_context         ,
	)                                      {
		this.assertOpen();
		const index = this.state.documentAddresses.get(addressKey(address));
		if (at === "current") {
			return index?.currentId === undefined ? undefined : clone(this.state.documents.get(index.currentId) .record);
		}
		for (const id of index?.ids ?? []) {
			const record = this.state.documents.get(id) .record;
			if (isAliveAt(record, at)) return clone(record);
		}
		return undefined;
	}

	async document(id            , at               , _context         )                                      {
		this.assertOpen();
		const stored = this.materializeDocument(id, at);
		return stored === undefined
			? undefined
			: {
					record: clone(stored.record),
					version: stored.version,
					value: clone(stored.value),
					deltasSinceBase: stored.deltasSinceBase,
				};
	}

	async scanDocuments(
		query               ,
		limit        ,
		cursor                    ,
		_context         ,
	)                                        {
		this.assertOpen();
		const ids = this.state.documentIdsByScope.get(scopeKey(query.scope)) ?? [];
		const after = cursorId(cursor);
		const start = after === undefined ? 0 : upperBound(ids, after);
		const values                   = [];
		for (let index = start; index < ids.length && values.length <= limit; index++) {
			const record = this.state.documents.get(ids[index] ) .record;
			if (query.kind !== undefined && record.kind !== query.kind) continue;
			if (isAliveAt(record, query.at)) values.push(record);
		}
		return page(values, limit);
	}

	async close(_context         )                {
		this.closed = true;
	}

	        materializeDocument(id            , at               )                             {
		const stored = this.state.documents.get(id);
		if (stored === undefined) return undefined;
		if (at !== "current" && isCurrentOnly(stored.record)) {
			throw new StorageRequestError(`Document ${id} does not retain historical content`);
		}
		if (!isAliveAt(stored.record, at)) return undefined;
		const revisions = at === "current" ? stored.revisions : stored.revisions.filter((revision) => revision.seq <= at);
		let baseIndex = revisions.length - 1;
		while (baseIndex >= 0 && revisions[baseIndex] .kind !== "base") baseIndex--;
		const base = revisions[baseIndex];
		if (base?.kind !== "base") throw new Error(`Document ${id} is missing a required base`);
		const value = applyImmutableBatches(
			base.value,
			documentDeltaBatches(id, base.version, revisions, baseIndex + 1),
		)              ;
		return { record: stored.record, version: base.version, value, deltasSinceBase: revisions.length - baseIndex - 1 };
	}

	;       *visibleEntries(
		conversationId                ,
		minEntryId         = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY,
		maxEntryId         = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
	)                         {
		if (!this.state.conversations.has(conversationId)) {
			throw new StorageRequestError(`Unknown conversation: ${conversationId}`);
		}
		let currentId = conversationId;
		let upperEntryId = maxEntryId;
		while (true) {
			const ids = this.state.entryIds.get(currentId) ?? [];
			for (let index = upperBound(ids, upperEntryId) - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
				const id = ids[index];
				if (id < minEntryId) break;
				yield this.state.entries.get(id) ;
			}
			const conversation = this.state.conversations.get(currentId) ;
			if (conversation.parent === undefined) break;
			upperEntryId = Math.min(upperEntryId, conversation.parent.at);
			if (upperEntryId < minEntryId) break;
			currentId = conversation.parent.conversationId;
		}
	}

	/** Visible entries oldest first: the fork chain's segments from the root conversation forward. */
	;       *visibleEntriesAscending(
		conversationId                ,
		minEntryId         = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY,
		maxEntryId         = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
	)                         {
		if (!this.state.conversations.has(conversationId)) {
			throw new StorageRequestError(`Unknown conversation: ${conversationId}`);
		}
		const segments                                                                        = [];
		let currentId = conversationId;
		let upperEntryId = maxEntryId;
		while (true) {
			segments.push({ conversationId: currentId, upper: upperEntryId });
			const conversation = this.state.conversations.get(currentId) ;
			if (conversation.parent === undefined) break;
			upperEntryId = Math.min(upperEntryId, conversation.parent.at);
			if (upperEntryId < minEntryId) break;
			currentId = conversation.parent.conversationId;
		}
		for (const segment of segments.reverse()) {
			const ids = this.state.entryIds.get(segment.conversationId) ?? [];
			for (let index = lowerBound(ids, minEntryId); index < ids.length; index++) {
				const id = ids[index] ;
				if (id > segment.upper) break;
				yield this.state.entries.get(id) ;
			}
		}
	}

	        checkGlobalIds(writes                         )       {
		const claimed = new Map                       ();
		for (const write of writes) {
			if (write.type === "document.change" || write.type === "document.retire") continue;
			const document = write.type === "document.create" || write.type === "document.copy";
			const table            = document ? "document" : write.type;
			const id = document ? write.record.id : write.value.id;
			const existing = tableContaining(this.state, id);
			const earlier = claimed.get(id);
			if (table === "conversation" || table === "entry" || table === "document") {
				if (existing !== undefined) throw new Error(`ID ${id} already belongs to ${existing}`);
				if (earlier !== undefined) throw new Error(`ID ${id} is written more than once`);
			} else {
				if (existing !== undefined && existing !== table) {
					throw new Error(`ID ${id} already belongs to ${existing}`);
				}
				if (earlier !== undefined && earlier !== table) throw new Error(`ID ${id} is written as two record types`);
			}
			claimed.set(id, table);
		}
	}

	        prepareDocumentActions(writes                         )                                  {
		const actions = new Map                            ();
		for (const write of writes) {
			if (write.type !== "document.create" && write.type !== "document.change" && write.type !== "document.retire") {
				continue;
			}
			const id = write.type === "document.create" ? write.record.id : write.id;
			let action = actions.get(id);
			if (action === undefined) {
				action = { retire: false };
				actions.set(id, action);
			}
			switch (write.type) {
				case "document.create":
					if (action.create !== undefined || action.content !== undefined) {
						throw new Error(`Document ${id} has more than one content command`);
					}
					action.create = write.record;
					action.content = write.content;
					break;
				case "document.change":
					if (action.content !== undefined) throw new Error(`Document ${id} has more than one content command`);
					action.content = write.content;
					break;
				case "document.retire":
					if (action.retire) throw new Error(`Document ${id} is retired more than once`);
